The multiplier-3.5 run completed. I’m recording metrics and deciding whether to keep the code/config change.

Multiplier 3.5 regressed, so I’m discarding that code/config change and returning to the multiplier-3 winner.

The architecture search keeps pointing back to the same best. I’m testing optimizer cost/quality next: reduce `muon_ns_steps` from 5 to 4, which makes each Muon orthogonalization cheaper and may improve fixed-time throughput.

Experiment `b4053dc` is running with `muon_ns_steps=4`. Best to beat is `0.976432`.
